--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY c.region
ORDER BY customers_region ASC;
--- exec

=== level4/09_allocate_by_measure.json
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;
--- exec
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC
OFFSET 0 ROWS FETCH NEXT 1000001 ROWS ONLY;
--- parameters String:FY27-Q1
--- duckdb
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC
LIMIT 1000001;
--- ssas_finance dax
EVALUATE
SELECTCOLUMNS (
    SUMMARIZECOLUMNS (
        'Customer'[Region],
        TREATAS ( { "FY27-Q1" }, 'Date'[Fiscal Quarter] ),
        "budget_budget_amount", [Budget Amount]
    ),
    "customers_region", 'Customer'[Region],
    "budget_budget_amount", [budget_budget_amount]
)
ORDER BY [customers_region] ASC
--- parameters String:FY27-Q1
--- duckdb
SELECT f.region AS customers_region,
       SUM(f.amount) AS budget_budget_amount
FROM fact_budget f
JOIN dim_date b ON b.calendar_date = f.month_start
WHERE b.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY f.region
ORDER BY customers_region ASC
LIMIT 1000001;

SELECTCOLUMNS (
    SUMMARIZECOLUMNS (

       SUM(f.amount) AS budget_budget_amount

--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       (GROUPING(c.region) + GROUPING(p.product)) AS _rollup_level,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY ROLLUP(c.region, p.product)
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;
--- exec
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       (GROUPING(c.region) + GROUPING(p.product)) AS _rollup_level,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY ROLLUP(c.region, p.product)
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC
OFFSET 0 ROWS FETCH NEXT 1000001 ROWS ONLY;
--- parameters String:FY27-Q1
--- duckdb
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       (GROUPING(c.region) + GROUPING(p.product)) AS _rollup_level,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY ROLLUP(c.region, p.product)
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC
LIMIT 1000001;
--- ssas_finance dax
EVALUATE
SELECTCOLUMNS (
    SUMMARIZECOLUMNS (
        ROLLUPADDISSUBTOTAL ( 'Customer'[Region], "_sub_customers_region" ),
        TREATAS ( { "FY27-Q1" }, 'Date'[Fiscal Quarter] ),
        "budget_budget_amount", [Budget Amount]
    ),
    "customers_region", 'Customer'[Region],
    "_rollup_level", IF ( [_sub_customers_region], 1, 0 ),
    "budget_budget_amount", [budget_budget_amount]
)
ORDER BY [customers_region] ASC
--- parameters String:FY27-Q1
--- duckdb
SELECT f.region AS customers_region,
       (GROUPING(f.region)) AS _rollup_level,
       SUM(f.amount) AS budget_budget_amount
FROM fact_budget f
JOIN dim_date b ON b.calendar_date = f.month_start
WHERE b.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY ROLLUP(f.region)
ORDER BY customers_region ASC
LIMIT 1000001;

=== level4/102_chain_rank_of_a_share_stays_remaining.json
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;
--- exec
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC
OFFSET 0 ROWS FETCH NEXT 1000001 ROWS ONLY;
--- parameters String:FY27-Q1
--- duckdb
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC
LIMIT 1000001;

--- sqlserver_sales tsql

       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;

SELECTCOLUMNS (
    SUMMARIZECOLUMNS (
        'Customer'[Region],
        TREATAS ( { "FY27-Q1" }, 'Date'[Fiscal Quarter] ),
        "budget_budget_amount", [Budget Amount]
    ),
    "customers_region", 'Customer'[Region],
    "budget_budget_amount", [budget_budget_amount]
)
ORDER BY [customers_region] ASC
--- parameters String:FY27-Q1
--- duckdb

       SUM(f.amount) AS budget_budget_amount
FROM fact_budget f
JOIN dim_date b ON b.calendar_date = f.month_start
WHERE b.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY f.region
ORDER BY customers_region ASC
LIMIT 1000001;

=== level4/111_index_on_a_datetime.json
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT d3.fiscal_month AS orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
LEFT JOIN dim_date d3 ON d3.calendar_date = CAST(CAST((f.ordered_at AT TIME ZONE 'UTC') AT TIME ZONE 'Central Standard Time' AS datetime2) AS date)
WHERE d3.fiscal_month IN (@p1, @p2)
GROUP BY d3.fiscal_month
ORDER BY orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month ASC;
--- exec

--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
  AND p.product = @p2
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;
--- exec

--- ssas_finance dax
EVALUATE
SELECTCOLUMNS (
    SUMMARIZECOLUMNS (
        'Customer'[Region],
        TREATAS ( { "FY27-Q1" }, 'Date'[Fiscal Quarter] ),
        "budget_budget_amount", [Budget Amount]
    ),
    "customers_region", 'Customer'[Region],
    "budget_budget_amount", [budget_budget_amount]
)
ORDER BY [customers_region] ASC
--- parameters String:FY27-Q1
--- duckdb

       SUM(f.amount) AS budget_budget_amount
FROM fact_budget f
JOIN dim_date b ON b.calendar_date = f.month_start
WHERE b.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY f.region
ORDER BY customers_region ASC
LIMIT 1000001;

--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       d.fiscal_month AS date_fiscal_month,
       (GROUPING(c.region) + GROUPING(d.fiscal_month)) AS _rollup_level,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_month IN (@p1, @p2)
GROUP BY ROLLUP(c.region, d.fiscal_month)
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, date_fiscal_month ASC;

       d.fiscal_month AS date_fiscal_month,
       (GROUPING(c.region) + GROUPING(d.fiscal_month)) AS _rollup_level,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_month IN (@p1, @p2)
GROUP BY ROLLUP(c.region, d.fiscal_month)
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, date_fiscal_month ASC
OFFSET 0 ROWS FETCH NEXT 1000001 ROWS ONLY;
--- parameters String:2026-07, String:2026-08
--- duckdb
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       d.fiscal_month AS date_fiscal_month,
       (GROUPING(c.region) + GROUPING(d.fiscal_month)) AS _rollup_level,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_month IN ($1, $2)
GROUP BY ROLLUP(c.region, d.fiscal_month)
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, date_fiscal_month ASC
LIMIT 1000001;

=== level4/13_allocate_equal_catalog.json
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
  AND p.product = @p2
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;
--- exec

--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       (GROUPING(c.region) + GROUPING(p.product)) AS _rollup_level,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY ROLLUP(c.region, p.product)
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;

JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY c.region, d.fiscal_quarter
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, date_fiscal_quarter ASC;

WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY d.fiscal_quarter, ROLLUP(c.region)
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, date_fiscal_quarter ASC;

SELECTCOLUMNS (
    SUMMARIZECOLUMNS (
        'Date'[Fiscal Quarter],
        TREATAS ( { "FY27-Q1" }, 'Date'[Fiscal Quarter] ),
        "budget_budget_amount", [Budget Amount]
    ),
    "date_fiscal_quarter", 'Date'[Fiscal Quarter],
    "budget_budget_amount", [budget_budget_amount]
)
ORDER BY [date_fiscal_quarter] ASC
--- parameters String:FY27-Q1
--- duckdb
SELECT b.fiscal_quarter AS date_fiscal_quarter,
       SUM(f.amount) AS budget_budget_amount
FROM fact_budget f
JOIN dim_date b ON b.calendar_date = f.month_start
WHERE b.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY b.fiscal_quarter
ORDER BY date_fiscal_quarter ASC
LIMIT 1000001;

       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;

       SUM(f.amount) AS budget_budget_amount
FROM fact_budget f
JOIN dim_date b ON b.calendar_date = f.month_start
WHERE b.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY f.region
ORDER BY customers_region ASC
LIMIT 1000001;
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       (GROUPING(c.region) + GROUPING(p.product)) AS _rollup_level,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY ROLLUP(c.region, p.product)
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;

GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;

=== level4/50_allocate_by_a_prior_year_in_the_weight_measures_role.json
--- ssas_finance dax
EVALUATE
SELECTCOLUMNS (
    SUMMARIZECOLUMNS (
        'Customer'[Region],
        TREATAS ( { "2026-08" }, 'Date'[Fiscal Month] ),
        "budget_budget_amount", [Budget Amount]
    ),
    "customers_region", 'Customer'[Region],
    "budget_budget_amount", [budget_budget_amount]
)
ORDER BY [customers_region] ASC
--- parameters String:2026-08
--- duckdb
SELECT f.region AS customers_region,
       SUM(f.amount) AS budget_budget_amount
FROM fact_budget f
JOIN dim_date b ON b.calendar_date = f.month_start
WHERE b.fiscal_month = $1
GROUP BY f.region
ORDER BY customers_region ASC
LIMIT 1000001;
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT d2.fiscal_month AS date_fiscal_month,
       d2.prior_year_fiscal_month AS date_fiscal_month_prior_year
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_date d2 ON d2.date_key = f.ship_date_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
WHERE d2.fiscal_month = @p1
  AND p.product = @p2
GROUP BY d2.fiscal_month, d2.prior_year_fiscal_month
ORDER BY date_fiscal_month ASC, date_fiscal_month_prior_year ASC;
--- parameters String:2026-08, String:A
--- duckdb
SELECT d2.fiscal_month AS date_fiscal_month,
       d2.prior_year_fiscal_month AS date_fiscal_month_prior_year
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_date d2 ON d2.date_key = f.ship_date_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
WHERE d2.fiscal_month = $1
  AND p.product = $2
GROUP BY d2.fiscal_month, d2.prior_year_fiscal_month
ORDER BY date_fiscal_month ASC, date_fiscal_month_prior_year ASC;

ORDER BY customers_region ASC;

=== level4/51_prior_period_of_a_datetime.json
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT d3.fiscal_month AS orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
LEFT JOIN dim_date d3 ON d3.calendar_date = CAST(CAST((f.ordered_at AT TIME ZONE 'UTC') AT TIME ZONE 'Central Standard Time' AS datetime2) AS date)
WHERE d3.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY d3.fiscal_month
ORDER BY orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month ASC;
--- exec

OFFSET 0 ROWS FETCH NEXT 1000001 ROWS ONLY;

=== level4/52_derived_across_two_facts.json
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;
--- exec

       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC
LIMIT 1000001;

JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;

=== level4/52_prior_period_over_dates_of_a_datetime.json
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT d3.fiscal_month AS orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
LEFT JOIN dim_date d3 ON d3.calendar_date = CAST(CAST((f.ordered_at AT TIME ZONE 'UTC') AT TIME ZONE 'Central Standard Time' AS datetime2) AS date)
WHERE (f.ordered_at >= CAST(@p1 AS datetime2) AND f.ordered_at < CAST(@p2 AS datetime2))
GROUP BY d3.fiscal_month
ORDER BY orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month ASC;
--- exec
SELECT d3.fiscal_month AS orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
LEFT JOIN dim_date d3 ON d3.calendar_date = CAST(CAST((f.ordered_at AT TIME ZONE 'UTC') AT TIME ZONE 'Central Standard Time' AS datetime2) AS date)
WHERE (f.ordered_at >= CAST(@p1 AS datetime2) AND f.ordered_at < CAST(@p2 AS datetime2))
GROUP BY d3.fiscal_month
ORDER BY orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month ASC
OFFSET 0 ROWS FETCH NEXT 1000001 ROWS ONLY;
--- parameters String:2026-07-01 05:00:00, String:2026-10-01 05:00:00
--- duckdb
SELECT d3.fiscal_month AS orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
LEFT JOIN dim_date d3 ON d3.calendar_date = CAST(timezone('America/Chicago', timezone('UTC', f.ordered_at)) AS DATE)
WHERE (f.ordered_at >= CAST($1 AS TIMESTAMP) AND f.ordered_at < CAST($2 AS TIMESTAMP))
GROUP BY d3.fiscal_month
ORDER BY orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month ASC
LIMIT 1000001;
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT d3.fiscal_month AS orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month,
       d3.prior_year_fiscal_month AS orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month_prior_year
FROM fact_sales f
LEFT JOIN dim_date d3 ON d3.calendar_date = CAST(CAST((f.ordered_at AT TIME ZONE 'UTC') AT TIME ZONE 'Central Standard Time' AS datetime2) AS date)
WHERE (f.ordered_at >= CAST(@p1 AS datetime2) AND f.ordered_at < CAST(@p2 AS datetime2))
GROUP BY d3.fiscal_month, d3.prior_year_fiscal_month
ORDER BY orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month ASC, orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month_prior_year ASC;
--- parameters String:2026-07-01 05:00:00, String:2026-10-01 05:00:00
--- duckdb
SELECT d3.fiscal_month AS orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month,
       d3.prior_year_fiscal_month AS orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month_prior_year
FROM fact_sales f
LEFT JOIN dim_date d3 ON d3.calendar_date = CAST(timezone('America/Chicago', timezone('UTC', f.ordered_at)) AS DATE)
WHERE (f.ordered_at >= CAST($1 AS TIMESTAMP) AND f.ordered_at < CAST($2 AS TIMESTAMP))
GROUP BY d3.fiscal_month, d3.prior_year_fiscal_month
ORDER BY orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month ASC, orders_ordered_at_fiscal_month_prior_year ASC;

=== level4/55_percent_of_a_ratio_is_not_allowed.json
=== level4/55_prior_period_a_year_later_over_dates.json
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT d.fiscal_month AS date_fiscal_month,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.calendar_date BETWEEN CAST(@p1 AS DATE) AND CAST(@p2 AS DATE)
GROUP BY d.fiscal_month
ORDER BY date_fiscal_month ASC;
--- exec
SELECT d.fiscal_month AS date_fiscal_month,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f

=== level4/93_chain_with_totals.json
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;
--- exec

       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue

JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY c.region, p.product
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC

--- parameters String:FY27-Q1
--- duckdb
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key

--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT c.region AS customers_region,
       p.product AS products_product,
       (GROUPING(c.region) + GROUPING(p.product)) AS _rollup_level,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS orders_net_revenue
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_customer c ON c.customer_key = f.customer_key
JOIN dim_product p ON p.product_key = f.product_key
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY ROLLUP(c.region, p.product)
ORDER BY customers_region ASC, products_product ASC;
--- exec

JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY d.fiscal_month, d.prior_year_fiscal_month
ORDER BY date_fiscal_month ASC, date_fiscal_month_prior_year ASC;
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT d.fiscal_quarter AS date_fiscal_quarter,
       d.prior_year_fiscal_quarter AS date_fiscal_quarter_prior_year
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY d.fiscal_quarter, d.prior_year_fiscal_quarter
ORDER BY date_fiscal_quarter ASC, date_fiscal_quarter_prior_year ASC;
--- parameters String:FY27-Q1
--- duckdb
SELECT d.fiscal_quarter AS date_fiscal_quarter,
       d.prior_year_fiscal_quarter AS date_fiscal_quarter_prior_year
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY d.fiscal_quarter, d.prior_year_fiscal_quarter
ORDER BY date_fiscal_quarter ASC, date_fiscal_quarter_prior_year ASC;
--- sqlserver_sales tsql
SELECT d.fiscal_month AS date_fiscal_month,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS rev_delta__prior
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY d.fiscal_month
ORDER BY date_fiscal_month ASC;
--- exec
SELECT d.fiscal_month AS date_fiscal_month,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS rev_delta__prior
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = @p1
GROUP BY d.fiscal_month
ORDER BY date_fiscal_month ASC
OFFSET 0 ROWS FETCH NEXT 1000001 ROWS ONLY;
--- parameters String:FY26-Q1
--- duckdb
SELECT d.fiscal_month AS date_fiscal_month,
       SUM(f.net_amount) AS rev_delta__prior
FROM fact_sales f
JOIN dim_date d ON d.date_key = f.date_key
WHERE d.fiscal_quarter = $1
GROUP BY d.fiscal_month
ORDER BY date_fiscal_month ASC
LIMIT 1000001;
